# Paczka AI P31 (Fala 2 · 2B) — Przedmioty, sklep, ekstrakcja, Przepustka Bitewna i UI wydarzeń (część 2/9)

Tymczasowy przekład interfejsu, opisów technicznych i kart meta/statystyk (`origin: ai`, zgodnie z `D-11`).
Obowiązuje słownik terminów w `paczki_ai/SLOWNIK_TERMINOW.md` oraz zasady formatowania z `docs/PAKIET_TLUMACZA.md`.
Po wypełnieniu zapisz plik jako `zwrotki/ai/P31_2b_przedmioty_sklep_wydarzenia_cz02.md` i zaimportuj poleceniem:
`python3 tools/import_zwrotki.py --ai zwrotki/ai/P31_2b_przedmioty_sklep_wydarzenia_cz02.md`.

- Zasady techniczne: nie zmieniaj linii `źródło:`, `słownik:`, `EN:` ani `(powtórzenie elementu …)`; zachowaj wszystkie tokeny `{0}`, `<color=…>`, `[OnHit]`, `<sprite name=…>`.
- Wpisy już przetłumaczone przez człowieka są chronione (nawet przy ponownym imporcie `--ai` nie zostaną nadpisane).

## Interfejs RPG: RPGSuicideBoxUI
- plik gry: `RPGSuicideBoxUI.json`

62. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_reset_button_text] — tekst] 재고정리\n(리셋)
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_reset_button_text].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Liquidation\n(Reset)
   PL: Liquidacja zapasów\n(Reset)
63. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_reset_confirm_desc] — tekst] 재고정리를 진행하시겠습니까?\n\n선택 시, 현재 진행 중인 회차의 기록이 초기화되며 복구할 수 없습니다.\n※진행 중 얻지 못한 아이템은\n이후 플레이에서 획득하실 수 있습니다.\n※재고정리를 하지 않아도 아이템은 전부 획득하실 수 있습니다.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_reset_confirm_desc].content
   limit: do 370 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Proceed with Liquidation?\n\nSelecting this option will reset your current run and cannot be reverted.\n※ Items that have not been obtained from this Part can be obtained in the next Part.\n※ You can still get all of the items without using resetting your run via the Liquidation function.
   PL: Przeprowadzić liquidację zapasów?\n\nPo wyborze zapis bieżącego przebiegu zostanie zresetowany i nie da się go odzyskać.\n※ Przedmioty, których nie udało się zdobyć w trakcie,
64. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_hardreset_button_text] — tekst] 폐점\n(하드 리셋)
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_hardreset_button_text].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Close Down\n(Hard Reset)
   PL: Zamknięcie firmy\n(Twardy reset)
65. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_hardreset_confirm_desc] — tekst] 폐점을 진행하시겠습니까?\n\n선택 시, 모든 회차의 기록이 초기화되며 복구할 수 없습니다.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_hardreset_confirm_desc].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Close down all Shops?\n\nSelecting this option will reset all runs and cannot be reverted.
   PL: Zamknąć wszystkie Sklepy?\n\nPo wyborze zapisy wszystkich przebiegów zostaną zresetowane i nie da się ich odzyskać.
66. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_main_screen_desc_default] — tekst] 스스로 목숨을 끊는 것은,\n이해치 못한 삶에 대한 고백과 다르지 않다.\n그렇다면 당신은 무엇으로 목숨을 끊어 삶에 바칠 것인가.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_main_screen_desc_default].content
   limit: do 200 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: To end one's own life is to confess that life was never truly understood.\nBy what means, then, will you kill yourself as a dedication to life itself?
   PL: Pożegnać się z własnym życiem\nto nie coś innego niż wyznanie,\nże życia nie dało się pojąć.\nCzym więc oddasz je, kończąc je?
67. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_coming_soon] — tekst] {0}월 {1}일 (KST) 업데이트 예정
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_coming_soon].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: {0}, {1}
   EN: To be updated on {0}-{1} (KST)
   PL: Planowana aktualizacja: {0}.{1} (KST)
68. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_1] — tekst] 생애 단 한 번뿐인 순간을, 더욱 선명하게.\n오늘도 저희 자살 자판기를 이용해 주셔서 감사합니다.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_1].content
   limit: do 180 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Get the most vivid experience of a once-in-a-lifetime moment.\nThank you for your continued patronage of our Suicide Vending Machines.
   PL: Jedyny w życiu moment, jeszcze wyraźniej.\nDziś również dziękujemy za skorzystanie\nz naszego Samobójczego Automatu.
69. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_2] — tekst] 본 모델은 가족, 친구, 연인 등 복수 인원이 함께 이용하는 공동 체험형 시설입니다.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_2].content
   limit: do 160 znaków
   EN: This model is a multi-user communal experience unit intended for use by groups such as families, friends, or couples.
   PL: Ten model to urządzenie do wspólnych przeżyć,\nprzeznaczone dla większej liczby osób: rodziny, przyjaciół, par.
70. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_3] — tekst] 대호수 조난, 열차 충돌, 약물 과다 중독, 낙뢰, 저체온증을 비롯한 일부 자살 체험은 선택하실 수 없습니다.\n보다 다양한 경험을 원하시는 고객께서는 일인용 자살 자판기를 이용해 주시기 바랍니다.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_3].content
   limit: do 380 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: This model does not support certain suicide experiences, including being stranded upon the Great Lake, railway collision, drug overdose, lightning strike, and death by hypothermia.\nFor an even wider variety of experiences, please visit one of our private single-guest Suicide Vending Machines.
   PL: Niektórych samobójczych doświadczeń — jak szkwał na wielkim jeziorze, katastrofa pociągu, przedawkowanie, porażenie piorunem, hipotermia — nie można wybrać.\nDla tych, którzy pragną bogatszych wrażeń
71. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_4] — tekst] 부담 없이 경험하는 전율적인 공포와 스릴.\n아래 버튼 중 하나를 눌러 특별한 죽음을 경험하세요.
   źródło: RPGSuicideBoxUI.json :: dataList[id=rpg_suicidebox_intro_ab_4].content
   limit: do 160 znaków

   PL: Dreszczyk grozy i emocji bez żadnego obciążenia.\nWciśnij jeden z poniższych przycisków i zaznaj specjalną śmierć.
72. [Interfejs RPG [rpg_suicidebox_pressed_dialog_ab] — tekst] 임산부, 노약자, 약물 사용자 및 심혈관계 질환자는 이용에 주의하세요.\n무리한 사용으로 인한 사고 발생 시, 모든 책임은 사용자 본인에게 있습니다.

## Interfejs i ustawienia: FAQ
- plik gry: `FAQ.json`

101. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_description] — tekst] 자주 묻는 질문을\n이곳에서 확인할 수 있습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_description].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: You can check\nFrequently Asked Questions here.
   PL: Najczęściej zadawane pytania\nmożna sprawdzić tutaj.
102. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment] — tekst] 결제
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Payment
   PL: Płatności
103. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_1] — tekst] 월간 광기 패키지 / 월간 광기 하프 패키지는 어떻게 사용되나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_1].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: How does the Monthly Lunacy Supply work?
   PL: Jak działa Miesięczny Pakiet Szaleństwa / Miesięczny Półpakiet Szaleństwa?
104. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_1] — tekst] <b>월간 광기 패키지</b>를 구매하신 경우, 구매 보상인 광기가 650만큼 지급되며, 구매일로부터 30일 동안 아래와 같은 일일 보상을 수령할 수 있습니다.<b>월간 광기 하프 패키지</b>를 구매하신 경우, 구매 보상인 광기가 130만큼 지급되며, 구매일로부터 30일 동안 아래와 같은 일일 보상을 수령할 수 있습니다.\n\n■ 월간 광기 패키지 일일 보상:\n무료 광기 65\n\n■ 월간 광기 하프 패키지 일일 보상:\n무료 광기 39\n\n\n※ 주의사항\n1) 모든 보상은 게임 내 우편으로 지급됩니다.\n2) 위 두 패키지는 별개로 적용됩니다.\n3) 두 패키지는 최대 2번 추가 구매 가능합니다. (최대 67일까지 추가 가능)\n4) 게임 미접속 시 해당 날짜의 보상은 지급되지 않습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_1].content
   limit: do 1000 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <b>, </b>, \n
   EN: When you purchase the <b>Monthly Lunacy Supply</b>, you will be given 650 Lunacy upfront, and you become eligible to receive the daily rewards listed below for the following 30 days.\nWhen you purchase the <b>Monthly Lunacy Batch</b>, you will be given 130 Lunacy upfront, and you become eligible to receive the daily rewards listed below for the following 30 days.\n\n■ Daily Rewards from Monthly Lunacy Supply:\n65 Free Lunacy\n\n■ Daily Rewards from Monthly Lunacy Batch:\n39 Free Lunacy\n\n※ NOTES\n1) All rewards are provided through in-game mail.\n2) The two monthly packs function separately.\n3) The two monthly packs can be purchased again up to two times within their duration. (This duration can be extended up to 67 days.)\n4) The daily rewards will not be provided for days when you do not log into the game.
   PL: Przy zakupie <b>Miesięcznego Pakietu Szaleństwa</b> otrzymujesz od razu 650 Szaleństwa, a przez 30 dni od dnia zakupu możesz odbierać codzienne nagrody jak poniżej. Przy zakupie <b>Miesięcznego Półpakietu Szaleństwa</b> otrzymujesz od razu 130 Szaleństwa, a przez 30 dni od dnia zakupu możesz odbierać codzienne nagrody jak poniżej.\n\n■ Codzienne nagrody Miesięcznego Pakietu Szaleństwa:\n65 darmowego Szaleństwa\n\n■ Codzienne nagrody Miesięcznego Półpakietu Szaleństwa:\n39 darmowego Szaleństwa\n\n\n※ Uwagi\n1) Wszystkie nagrody trafiają do gry pocztą.\n2) Oba pakiety działają niezależnie.\n3) Oba pakiety można dokupić maksymalnie 2 razy. (maks. wydłużenie do 67 dni)\n4) W dni bez zalogowania nagroda danego dnia nie zostanie wydana.
105. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_2] — tekst] 결제된 상품을 환불하고 싶어요. 방법이 어떻게 되나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_2].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: I want to refund an item I purchased. How do I?
   PL: Chcę zwrócić zakupiony przedmiot. Jak to zrobić?
106. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_2] — tekst] 결제 상품 환불의 경우, 결제 후 7일 이내 문의 접수 및 지급된 아이템을 사용하지 않으셨을 경우에만 결제 취소가 가능합니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_2].content
   limit: do 130 znaków
   EN: You may request a refund within 7 days of purchase as long as you did not use the purchased item.
   PL: Zwrot jest możliwy, jeśli zgłoszono w ciągu 7 dni od zakupu i nabyty przedmiot nie został użyty.
107. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_3] — tekst] 환불이 가능한 결제 상품은 어떤 것들이 있나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_3].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: What are purchases I am able to refund?
   PL: Które zakupy można zwrócić?
108. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_3] — tekst] 각종 패키지, 기간제 상품, 보너스 포함 상품 등의 경우 수령 즉시 효과가 적용된 것으로 간주되어 부득이하게 환불 진행에 도움을 드리기 어려운 점 양해 부탁드립니다.\n그 외에는 구매일로부터 14일이 지나지 않았으며, 수령 이후 사용하지 않은 상품에 한해 결제 상품 환불을 진행하실 수 있습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_3].content
   limit: do 370 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Please understand that certain packs, period-based products, items that include bonuses, and other products that come immediately into effect upon purchase may be difficult to refund.\nOtherwise, you may request a refund for items within 14 days of purchase as long as you did not use them.
   PL: Pakiety, przedmioty okresowe, przedmioty z bonusami itp. uznaje się za użyte natychmiast po odbiorze, dlatego niestety nie możemy pomóc w ich zwrocie. Mamy nadzieję na wyrozumiałość
109. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_4] — tekst] 결제한 내역이나 영수증은 어디서 확인할 수 있나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_4].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Where can I see my purchase history or receipts?
   PL: Gdzie mogę sprawdzić historię zakupów i paragony?
110. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_4] — tekst] <b>구글 결제를 통한 확인 방법</b>\n1. 구글 결제센터(<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/">https://payments.google.com/</link></u></color>)에 접속\n2. 결제한 구글 계정으로 로그인\n3. 구매 내역 보기 선택\n4. 확인하고 싶은 내역 선택\n5. 거래 ID 확인 (GPA. 0000-0000-0000-00000의 형태)\n\n<b>앱스토어를 통한 확인 방법</b>\n1. 아이튠즈 페이지(<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/">https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/</link></u></color>) 접속\n2. 로그인 후 [계정] 항목 선택\n3. 구매 내역에서 [모두 보기] 선택\n\n<b>스팀을 통한 확인 방법</b>\n1. 고객센터 페이지(<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>)접속\n2. 구매 항목 선택\n3. 전체 구매 기록 보기 선택\n4. 확인하고 싶은 내역 선택
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_4].content
   limit: do 710 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <b>, </b>, <color=#F8C200>, <u>, <link="https://payments.google.com/">, </link>, </u>, </color>, <link="https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/">, <link="https://help.steampowered.com/">, \n
   EN: <b>Using Google Pay</b>\n1. Enter Google Payment center (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/">https://payments.google.com/</link></u></color>)\n2. Log in with the Google account used to make the purchase\n3. Go to [Activity]\n4. Select the transaction to examine\n5. Check transaction ID (GPA. 0000-0000-0000-00000)\n\n<b>Using App Store</b>\n1. Enter iTunes page (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/">https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/</link></u></color>)\n2. Log in and go to [Account]\n3. Select [View All] in purchase history\n\n<b>Using Steam</b>\n1. Enter customer support page (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>)\n2. Go to [Purchases]\n3. Go to [View complete purchasing history]\n4. Check the transaction
   PL: <b>Sposób weryfikacji w Google Play</b>\n1. Wejdź do Centrum płatności Google (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/">https://payments.google.com/</link></u></color>)\n2. Zaloguj się na konto Google użyte do zakupu\n3. Wybierz „Aktywność”\n4. Wybierz transakcję do sprawdzenia\n5. Sprawdź ID transakcji (format GPA. 0000-0000-0000-00000)\n\n<b>Sposób weryfikacji w App Store</b>\n1. Wejdź na stronę iTunes (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/">https://www.apple.com/kr/itunes/</link></u></color>)\n2. Po zalogowaniu wybierz [Konto]\n3. W historii zakupów wybierz [Pokaż wszystko]\n\n<b>Sposób weryfikacji w Steam</b>\n1. Wejdź na stronę pomocy (<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>)\n2. Wybierz kategorię Zakupy\n3. Wybierz [Pokaż pełną historię zakupów]\n4. Wybierz transakcję do sprawdzenia
111. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_5] — tekst] 동일한 상품이 중복 결제되었어요.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_5].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Multiple payments were made for one item.
   PL: Ten sam przedmiot został opłacony kilkukrotnie.
112. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_5] — tekst] 상품이 중복으로 결제되었다면, 결제 영수증과 결제 내역을 포함하여 게임 내 고객센터로 문의를 보내주시기 바랍니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_5].content
   limit: do 230 znaków
   EN: If you encounter an error where multiple payments are made for a single product, please send an inquiry to customer service with the purchase history and the receipt included.
   PL: Jeśli przedmiot został opłacony kilkukrotnie, wyślij zapytanie do działu obsługi klienta w grze wraz z paragonem i historią transakcji.
113. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_6] — tekst] 결제했는데 아직 상품이 없어요. 어떻게 해야하나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_6].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: I made a purchase, but the item hasn’t arrived. What should I do?
   PL: Zapłaciłem, ale przedmiotu wciąż nie ma. Co robić?
114. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_6] — tekst] 게임 아이템을 구매하셨으나, 오류로 지급받지 못한 경우 게임 내 고객센터로 결제 영수증을 포함하여 문의 보내주시기 바랍니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_6].content
   limit: do 210 znaków
   EN: If you have encountered an error where you failed to receive in-game items from your purchase, please send an inquiry to customer service with the receipt included.
   PL: Jeśli kupiłeś przedmiot w grze, ale z powodu błędu nie został wydany, wyślij zapytanie do działu obsługi klienta w grze wraz z paragonem zakupu.
115. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_7] — tekst] 결제가 계속 실패하는데 이유가 뭘까요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_7].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: My payment keeps failing to process, why is that?
   PL: Płatność ciągle nie przechodzi. Dlaczego?
116. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_7] — tekst] 현재 게임 내에서 구매할 수 있는 결제 상품은 플레이 스토어(구글)와 앱스토어를 통해 판매되고 있으며, 결제 수단도 다양하기에 원인이 다양하게 있을 수 있습니다.\n\n■ 결제 실패 해결 방법\n1) 원활한 네트워크 환경에서 게임 종료 후 재실행\n2) 계정 설정 문제 발생 (구글 결제 고객센터 혹은 애플 고객 지원에 문의 필요) \n\n구글 고객지원 홈페이지\n링크 : <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase">https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase</link></u></color>\n\n애플 고객지원 홈페이지\n링크 : <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action">https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action</link></u></color>\n\nSTEAM 고객지원 홈페이지\n링크 : <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>\n\n3) 통신사 결제 문제 발생 (통신사로 문의)\n- 소액 결제 결제 한도 등 결제 수단의 문제가 있다면, 이용하는 카드사 또는 통신사 고객센터를 통해 확인하실 수 있어요.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_7].content
   limit: do 920 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <color=#F8C200>, <u>, <link="https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase">, </link>, </u>, </color>, <link="https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action">, <link="https://help.steampowered.com/">, \n
   EN: The in-game items are currently sold through the Google Play Store and App Store, taking a variety of payment methods; there may be various causes for the issue.\n\n■ Possible Solutions for Failed Transactions\n1) Restart the game under a stable network connection\n2) For problems with the account setting: \n\nGoogle Customer Support\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase">https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase</link></u></color>\n\nApple Customer Support\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action">https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action</link></u></color>\n\nSteam Customer Support\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>\n\n3) For problems with processing the payment:\n- For issues with the payment method such as budget limits, you may consult customer support for the card company or the network provider.
   PL: Przedmioty dostępne w grze są sprzedawane przez Play Store (Google) i App Store; przyczyn nieudanych płatności może być wiele, bo form płatności jest też wiele.\n\n■ Sposoby rozwiązania problemu z płatnością\n1) Uruchom grę ponownie w stabilnym środowisku sieciowym\n2) Przy problemach z ustawieniami konta:\n\nStrona wsparcia Google\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase">https://payments.google.com/payments/u/2/home#oneTimePurchase</link></u></color>\n\nStrona wsparcia Apple\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action">https://expresslane.apple.com/GetproductgroupList.action</link></u></color>\n\nStrona wsparcia STEAM\nLink: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/">https://help.steampowered.com/</link></u></color>\n\n3) Przy problemach z realizacją płatności:\n- Przy problemach z metodą płatności, jak limity mikropłatności, skontaktuj się z obsługą firmy kartowej lub operatora.
117. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_title_8] — tekst] 상품의 금액과 청구된 결제금액이 달라요.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_title_8].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: An item’s listed price doesn’t match how much it actually costs.
   PL: Cena przedmiotu różni się od obciążonej kwoty.
118. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_desc_8] — tekst] Limbus Company에서 구매할 수 있는 유료 상품은 각 스토어(구글 플레이스토어, 앱스토어, STEAM)의 정책에 따라 판매되고 있습니다. 유료 상품 구입 시, 상품에 표기된 가격을 기준으로 결제됩니다.\n\n다만, 결제수단에 따라 외화 결제가 진행될 경우, 환율 및 일부 수수료가 추가되어 상품에 기재된 금액과 다소 차이가 있을 수 있는 점 참고 부탁드립니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_desc_8].content
   limit: do 450 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Paid products in Limbus Company follow the policies of the respective store (Google Play Store, App Store, Steam) on which the game is hosted. The price of the items is as displayed.\n\nHowever, please note that there may be minor differences in the sum of payment due to external factors such as exchange rates and fees depending on the means of payment.
   PL: Płatne przedmioty w Limbus Company są sprzedawane zgodnie z zasadami danego sklepu (Google Play, App Store, STEAM). Różnice w kwotach mogą wynikać z kursów walut, podatków i opłat sklepu lub operatora płatności. Sprawdź szczegóły w sklepie.
119. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_payment_common_template] — tekst] ■ ANDROID 문의 양식\n1) UID(유저 번호):\n2) 요청 사항 : 결제 취소\n3) 구글 결제 계정 : (이메일 형식)\n4) 결제 일시 :\n5) 결제 상품명/금액 :\n6) 주문번호 : (또는 주문번호가 포함된 구글 영수증 스크린샷 첨부)\n\n■ IOS 문의 양식\n1) UID(유저 번호) :\n2) 요청 사항 : 결제 취소 / 상품 재지급\n3) 주문번호가 확인되는 아이튠즈 결제 내역 스크린샷 첨부 (필수)4) 결제 일시 : \n5) 결제 상품명/금액 : \n\n■ STEAM 문의 양식\n1) UID(유저 번호) :\n2) 요청 사항 : 결제 취소 \n3) 주문번호가 확인되는 STEAM 결제 내역 스크린샷 첨부 (필수)\n4) 결제 일시 : \n5) 결제 상품명/금액 :
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_payment_common_template].content
   limit: do 980 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: ■ Inquiry Template for Android\n1) UID (User Identification):\n2) Request: Payment cancellation\n3) Google Payment Account: (Enter email here)\n4) Date of Purchase:\n5) Purchased Item and Price Paid:\n6) Transaction ID: (Or a screenshot of the Google receipt with the transaction ID visible)\n\n■ Inquiry Template for iOS\n1) UID (User Identification):\n2) Request: Payment cancellation / Reissuance of item\n3) Screenshot of Purchase Record on iTunes with Identifiable Transaction ID (Mandatory)\n4) Date of Purchase: \n5) Purchased Item and Price Paid: \n\n■ Inquiry Template for STEAM\n1) UID (User Identification):\n2) Request: Payment cancellation\n3) Screenshot of Purchase Record on Steam with Identifiable Transaction ID (Mandatory)\n4) Date of Purchase: \n5) Purchased Item and Price Paid:
   PL: ■ Formularz zgłoszenia ANDROID\n1) UID (numer użytkownika):\n2) Sprawa: anulowanie zakupu\n3) Konto płatności Google: (format e-mail)\n4) Data i godzina płatności:\n5) Nazwa produktu:\n6) Zrzut ekranu paragonu:
120. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account] — tekst] 계정
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Account
   PL: Konto
121. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_1] — tekst] 계정 ID는 어디서 확인할 수 있나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_1].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Where can I check my account ID?
   PL: Gdzie mogę sprawdzić ID konta?
122. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_1] — tekst] 계정 ID는 게임 내 하단 UI의 레벨 위에서 확인하실 수 있습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_1].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: The account ID is displayed above the level in the lower side of the screen.
   PL: ID konta wyświetla się nad poziomem w dolnym interfejsie gry.
123. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_2] — tekst] 계정 연동하는 방법이 궁금해요.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_2].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: I want to know how to link my account.
   PL: Jak połączyć konto?
124. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_2] — tekst] 환경 설정 → 기타 탭 → 계정란에서 연동이 가능합니다.\n\n게스트 계정으로 생성하였더라도 연동 완료 시 해당 계정으로 변경되는 점 참고 부탁드리며, 연동하지 않은 계정의 경우 데이터가 소실될 수 있는 점 유의해 주시기 바랍니다.\n\n※ 주의사항\n계정 유실을 방지하기 위해, 1회 이상 연동이 진행되었던 경우 최소 1개 이상의 연동 계정이 유지되도록 설정되어 있습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_2].content
   limit: do 480 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: To link your account, go to Settings → Other → Account.\n\nIf you use a guest account, it will be converted to a registered account after linking. Please note that unlinked accounts are susceptible to data loss when immediate access is lost.\n\n※ NOTE\nTo prevent loss of account data, accounts linked at least once must maintain linkage with at least 1 external service at a time.
   PL: Powiązanie można wykonać w Ustawienia → zakładka Inne → sekcja Konto.\n\nJeśli używasz konta gościa, po powiązaniu zostanie ono zamienione na powiązane konto. Bez powiązania nie można przenieść konta na inne urządzenie ani odzyskać go po reinstalacji.
125. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_3] — tekst] 계정을 잊어버렸어요. / 비밀번호를 잊어버렸어요.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_3].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: I forgot my account ID and/or the password.
   PL: Zapomniałem konta. / Zapomniałem hasła.
126. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_3] — tekst] 연동한 계정의 아이디나 비밀번호를 잊어버리셨을 경우, 선생님께서 연동한 계정의 고객센터에 문의하시어 확인 부탁 드리겠습니다.\n\n구글 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\n애플 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\n스팀 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>\n\n※주의사항\n게스트 계정의 경우 기기 변경, 애플리케이션 삭제등이 진행되었을 시 모든 계정 관련 데이터가 소실될 수 있는 점 유의해 주시기 바랍니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_3].content
   limit: do 690 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <color=#F8C200>, <u>, <link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">, </link>, </u>, </color>, <link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">, <link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">, \n
   EN: If you cannot remember the ID or the password of the account you linked your game account to, please contact the customer support of the respective service.\n\nGoogle Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\nApple Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\nSteam Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>\n\n※ NOTE\nPlease be aware that unlinked guest accounts may be permanently lost upon migrating devices or deleting the application.
   PL: Jeśli nie pamiętasz ID lub hasła powiązanego konta, skontaktuj się z obsługą klienta usługi, do której konto powiązałeś.\n\nObsługa Google: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\nObsługa Apple: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\nObsługa Steam: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>\n\n※ Uwaga\nPamiętaj, że w przypadku konta gościa zmiana urządzenia, usunięcie aplikacji itp. może spowodować utratę wszystkich danych konta.
127. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_4] — tekst] 기기 변경 및 재설치 이후에도 계정을 사용할 수 있나요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_4].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Can I keep using my account after changing to a different device or reinstalling the game?
   PL: Czy mogę dalej grać na koncie po zmianie urządzenia lub reinstalacji?
128. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_4] — tekst] 기기를 변경하였거나, 애플리케이션을 삭제 후 재설치하였더라도 기존의 계정으로 로그인하면 계속해서 게임을 진행하실 수 있습니다.\n\n다만, 게스트 계정 생성 이후 별도의 연동을 진행하지 않았다면 기기 변경 및 재설치 시 데이터가 소실될 수 있는 점 유의 부탁드립니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_4].content
   limit: do 360 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Even if you migrate to a different device or delete and then reinstall the application, you can log in with an existing account to continue playing.\n\nHowever, please note that guest accounts that did not undergo any integration may be lost when access to the application is lost.
   PL: Nawet po zmianie urządzenia albo usunięciu i ponownej instalacji aplikacji możesz grać dalej, logując się na istniejące konto.\n\nPamiętaj jednak, że konto gościa po reinstalacji lub zmianie urządzenia nie zostanie odzyskane. Zalecamy powiązanie konta.
129. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_5] — tekst] 계정을 삭제(탈퇴)하라면 어떻게 해야하나요
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_5].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: How do I delete (close) my account?
   PL: Jak usunąć (opuścić) konto?
130. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_5] — tekst] 계정 삭제(탈퇴)의 경우 계정 연동이 되어 있는 상태에서만 가능합니다.\n게스트 계정은 계정 연동하신 후 게임 탈퇴를 진행하실 수 있습니다.\n게임 내에서 설정 → ‘기타’ → ‘계정 탈퇴’를 선택하여 계정 삭제(탈퇴)를 진행할 수 있습니다.\n\n※ 계정 삭제 시 유예기간은 14일로 적용되며, 유예기간이 종료되면 계정 삭제가 완료됩니다.\n※ 계정 삭제를 하시면 게임 정보가 모두 사라지니, 유의하여 진행해주시기 바랍니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_5].content
   limit: do 520 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: You may only delete (close) an account that is linked with another service.\nGuest accounts also need to be linked before closure.\nTo delete (close) an account, enter Settings → ‘Other’ → ‘Close Account’.\n\n※ When you choose to close the account, you will be given a 14-day grace period, after which the closure is fully complete.\n※ All game data will be lost when the account is deleted, so proceed with caution.
   PL: Usunięcie konta (opuszczenie gry) jest możliwe tylko w stanie powiązania konta.\nKonto gościa najpierw trzeba powiązać, a potem opuścić grę.\nW grze: Ustawienia → zakładka Konto → Usuń konta.
131. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_6] — tekst] 혹시나 탈퇴한 계정은 복구가 가능한가요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_6].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Can a closed account be recovered?
   PL: Czy usunięte konto da się odzyskać?
132. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_6] — tekst] 게임 탈퇴 후 유예기간(14일)이 지나기 전, 기존에 이용하시던 계정으로 다시 로그인 하시면 탈퇴 복구를 하실 수 있습니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_6].content
   limit: do 130 znaków
   EN: If you log into the account before the 14-day grace period ends, you can restore the account.
   PL: Jeśli zalogujesz się na dotychczasowe konto przed upływem okresu karencji (14 dni), konto można przywrócić.
133. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_7] — tekst] 계정이 차단되었어요. 왜 그런가요?
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_title_7].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: My account was suspended. Why is that?
   PL: Moje konto zostało zablokowane. Dlaczego?
134. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_7] — tekst] 쾌적한 게임 환경을 제공하기 위해 운영정책에 따라 서비스를 제공하고 있습니다.\n운영정책에 위배되는 활동으로 다른 관리자분들 및 게임 서비스에 피해를 줄 경우 게임 이용이 제한 될 수 있습니다.\n\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://limbuscompany.kr/policy/">[게임 운영정책 바로 가기]</link></u></color>\n\n운영정책에 따라 게임 이용이 제한되었다면 별도의 조치로 도움을 드릴 수 없는 점 양해 부탁드립니다.
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_7].content
   limit: do 440 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <color=#F8C200>, <u>, <link="https://limbuscompany.kr/policy/">, </link>, </u>, </color>, \n
   EN: To provide an enjoyable experience, we operate under Terms of Service.\nMisconduct and violations of the terms that negatively affect the service and its management may result in restrictions from playing the game.\n\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://limbuscompany.kr/policy/">[Read full text]</link></u></color>\n\nPlease understand that we are unable to help you with restrictions carried out according to the Terms of Service.
   PL: Aby zapewnić sprzyjające środowisko gry, działamy zgodnie z zasadami eksploatacji.\nDziałania naruszające zasady, które wyrządzą szkodę innym graczom lub usłudze, mogą skutkować ograniczeniem grania.\n\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="https://limbuscompany.kr/policy/">[Zasady eksploatacji gry]</link></u></color>\n\nJeśli konto ograniczono zgodnie z zasadami, nie możemy pomóc dodatkowymi działaniami. Mamy nadzieję na wyrozumiałość.
135. [Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_title_8] — tekst] 계정 연동 실패 해결 방법

[Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_account_desc_8] — tekst] 연동한 계정에 문제가 발생하였을 경우 계정 도용 여부를 확인할 수 없어 도움을 드릴 수 없으며, 연동한 계정에 문제가 발생했다면 아래 고객센터에 문의해 주시기 바랍니다.\n\n구글 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\n애플 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\n스팀 고객센터: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>
   źródło: FAQ.json :: dataList[id=faq_account_desc_8].content
   limit: do 660 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: <color=#F8C200>, <u>, <link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">, </link>, </u>, </color>, <link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">, <link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">, \n
   EN: Please understand that we are unable to provide help regarding issues with external accounts linked to the user’s game account as we are unable to ensure that the access is not fraudulent. In such cases, please contact the customer support of the respective service.\n\nGoogle Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\nApple Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\nSteam Customer Support: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>
   PL: Gdy problem dotyczy powiązanego konta, nie możemy pomóc, bo nie da się zweryfikować, czy nie doszło do kradzieży konta. W takim wypadku skontaktuj się z obsługą klienta poniżej.\n\nObsługa Google: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296">https://support.google.com/accounts/#topic=3382296</link></u></color>\nObsługa Apple: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://support.apple.com/ko-kr">https://support.apple.com/ko-kr</link></u></color>\nObsługa Steam: <color=#F8C200><u><link="https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In">https://help.steampowered.com/ko/wizard/HelpWithPurchase.Vender+cromos+raacutepidamente+con+Steam+In</link></u></color>

[Interfejs i ustawienia [faq_normal_desc_1] — tekst] FAQ(자주묻는 질문)에 없는 문의는 고객센터 이메일로 문의 접수부탁드립니다.\n\n계정문의\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+account@projmoon.com">limbussupport+account@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\n결제문의\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com">limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\n버그제보\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com">limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\n앱 이용 문제\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com">limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\n기타 문의 사항\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+others@projmoon.com">limbussupport+others@projmoon.com</link></u></color>

   EN: For inquiries not covered in the FAQ, please contact our Customer Service emails listed below.\n\nAccount Inquiry\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+account@projmoon.com">limbussupport+account@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nPayment Inquiry\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com">limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nBug Report\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com">limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nIssues with application usage\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com">limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nOther Inquiries\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+others@projmoon.com">limbussupport+others@projmoon.com</link></u></color>
   PL: Sprawy nieujęte w FAQ (najczęstszych pytaniach) prosimy zgłaszać mailem do obsługi klienta.\n\nSprawy kont\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+account@projmoon.com">limbussupport+account@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nSprawy płatności\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com">limbussupport+payment@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nZgłoszenia błędów\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com">limbussupport+bug@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nProblemy z aplikacją\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com">limbussupport+applicationissue@projmoon.com</link></u></color>\n\nInne sprawy\n<color=#F8C200><u><link="mailto:limbussupport+others@projmoon.com">limbussupport+others@projmoon.com</link></u></color>

## Interfejs i ustawienia: FileDownloadDesc
- plik gry: `FileDownloadDesc.json`

140. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_01] — tytuł] 도시, 그리고 둥지
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_01].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: The City and the Nests
   PL: Miasto i Gniazda
141. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_01] — opis] 관리자 님이 처해 있는 특수한 상황을 저희는 이해하고 있습니다. 대부분 당연할 사실이지만, 이곳에 기술하도록 하려고 합니다.\n잊으셨을 지 모르지만, 우리는 26개의 구로 나뉘어진 거대한 도시 속에서 살아가고 있습니다. 모든 구역은 둥지라고 불리 우는 핵심 구역이 존재합니다. 새가 둥지 속 알을 품듯, 해당 둥지를 관장하는 거대 기업은 둥지 속 거주민을 보호합니다.\n물론, 모든 빛에 그림자가 있듯이 둥지의 바깥… 뒷골목의 거주민까지 보호하지는 않습니다.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_01].desc
   limit: do 720 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: We understand the unique situation you find yourself in, Executive Manager. Allow us to lay down a number of key facts about our world here.\nIn case you’ve become unfamiliar with your surroundings, we live in a massive City, divided into 26 Districts. Each District has a central area called the ‘Nest’, whose inhabitants are under the protection of a corporate entity overseeing the District, much like a bird brooding its eggs.\nAlas, the corporation’s protection doesn’t extend beyond the Nest’s boundaries… The dwellers of the Backstreets have to fend for themselves.
   PL: Rozumiemy wyjątkową sytuację, w której się znalazłeś, Panie Menedżerze. To oczywiste fakty, ale chcemy je tu opisać.\nMoże nie pamiętasz, ale jesteśmy 26 Kangurami w jednym ciele...
142. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_02] — tytuł] 날개
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_02].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: The Wings
   PL: Skrzydła
143. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_02] — opis] 새의 날개를 말하는 것이 아닙니다.\n하지만 둥지를 품는 어미 새와도 같으니, 관련성까지 부정할 수는 없겠습니다.\n날개는 도시의 각 둥지를 관장하는 26개의 초 거대 기업을 말합니다. 각 날개는 특이점이라고 불리는 놀라운 기술을 토대로 도시 곳곳의 삶의 질을 높이고, 둥지의 안녕을 도모하고 있다고 알려져 있습니다.\n그러나 날개가 꺾이기라도 하는 날에는 이야기가 달라집니다. 대대로, 도시의 날개가 꺾이는 날에는 어마어마한 사건들이 동반되어 왔죠.\n날개가 꺾인 자리에는 언젠가 새 날개가 돋아나기 마련입니다. 하지만, 그 동안 비어 버린 둥지의 안전은… 누구도 보장하지 못할 것입니다.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_02].desc
   limit: do 940 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: This term does not refer to a bird’s bodily structure.\nHowever, one can’t deny that a Wing’s role resembles that of a bird caring for its nest.\nThe ‘Wings’ are a collective term we use to refer to the 26 biggest conglomerates in the City. These Wings contribute to the quality of life in the City via astounding technological feats known as ‘Singularities’. Under the jurisdiction of a Wing, the citizens of each Nest enjoy comfortable and safe lives. However, that changes the moment a Wing is snapped or plucked of its feathers; historically, the fall of a Wing has accompanied events of considerable magnitudes.\nIn time, a new Wing will rise to fill that vacant spot. In the meantime, though… no one can guarantee your safety in its fallen Nest.
   PL: Nie chodzi tu o skrzydła ptaka.\nAle gdyby matka-ptak nosiła w sobie gniazdo, nie da się zaprzeczyć pewnemu pokrewieństwu.\nSkrzydła to 26 korporacji nadzorujących poszczególne Gniazda Miasta
144. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_03] — tytuł] 로보토미 코퍼레이션
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_03].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: Lobotomy Corporation
   PL: Lobotomy Corporation
145. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_03] — opis] 한 때 날개 중 하나 였던 L 둥지의 회사입니다.\nL사라고도 불렸던 그들은, 대량의 에너지를 생산해 도시의 전력을 담당하는 역할을 하고 있었습니다.\n그러나 백야, 흑주 사건이라고 불리우는 충격적인 사건을 계기로 회사는 몰락해 버렸으며, 지금도 L 둥지의 날개는 여전히 공석인 채 입니다.\n구 L사의 전력망은 도시를 아울렀기 때문에, 이곳저곳에 그들의 지부가 많이 세워져 있습니다. 몰락과 함께 그들도 전부 폐허로 변해버렸지만, 그 안에는 쓸 만한 것들이 남아있을 것입니다.\n어쩌면… 가장 위험한 것들과 함께.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_03].desc
   limit: do 770 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: A former Wing that was located in Nest L.\nLobotomy Corporation, or L Corp. (LC) for short, was an energy company that produced an ample supply of power for the City. The corporation has fallen since the ‘White Nights and Dark Days’ incident, and the spot of Nest L’s Wing remains unclaimed to this day.\nBecause the former L Corp’s electric grids had encompassed the City, its branches were built in various places. Though these branch facilities have all fallen to ruin with the collapse of the Wing, you should still be able to find valuable goods.\nAlong with… possibly the most dangerous beings you’ll face.
   PL: Korporacja, która kiedyś była jednym ze Skrzydeł w Gnieździe L.\nZwana też L Corp., produkowała ogromne ilości energii i odpowiadała za zasilanie Miasta.\nJednak noc „Białych Sennych Spojrzeń i Polegania", która zakończyła jej istnienie
146. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_04] — tytuł] 황금가지
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_04].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: The Golden Boughs
   PL: Złote Gałęzie
147. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_04] — opis] 몰락한 로보토미 코퍼레이션 지부의 지하에는 한 때 그들 기술의 정수였던 황금가지가 있습니다. 황금빛으로 빛나는, 나무의 가지죠.\n관리자께서, 수감자들과 함께 회수해야 할 가장 핵심적인 목표라고 할 수 있습니다.\n황금가지가 갖고 있는 무수한 가능성은 도시의 사람이라면 누구나 탐을 내기 때문에, 편의점에서 물건을 꺼내 오는 것처럼 쉬운 일이 되지는 않을 것입니다.\n현명한 판단을 통해 모든 가지를 회수해 오시기를 요청 드리는 바입니다.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_04].desc
   limit: do 750 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Within the depths of these facilities lie the Golden Boughs, the essence of Lobotomy Corporation’s energy production technology… or what’s left of it. Its appearance is exactly as the name suggests: a tree branch radiating a golden glow.\nYour objective as an Executive Manager is to obtain the Golden Boughs.\nOf course, any denizen of the City would be attracted by the promise of the infinite possibilities these items possess; the task of retrieval will not be as easy as grabbing goods from a convenience store.\nWe implore you to collect all the Golden Boughs with prudent decision-making.
   PL: W podziemiach upadłego oddziału Lobotomy Corporation leżą Złote Gałęzie — kiedyś kwintesencja ich technologii. Promieniste złotem gałęzie drzewa.\nPanie Menedżerze, odzyskaj je ze Skazańcami
148. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_05] — tytuł] 환상체
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_05].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: Abnormalities
   PL: Anomalie
149. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_05] — opis] 로보토미 코퍼레이션은 환상체라고 하는 것들을 이용하여 에너지를 생산했습니다.\n이름 그대로, 그것들은 실존한다고는 믿기지 않는 괴기하고 공포스러운 존재부터, 겉은 멀쩡하지만 잘못 ‘관리’ 했을 때 끔찍한 결말을 초래하는 존재까지 다양한 종류가 그 회사에 있었습니다.\n몰락한 이후, 대부분의 지부는 규정에 따라 (아마 직원은 제대로 알고 있지도 않았을, 극히 세부적인 조항이었겠지요.) 매몰되어 환상체와 직원들이 함께 갇히게 되었습니다.\n시간이 꽤 지났기에, 내부의 직원은 이미 명을 달리 했겠지만, 그것들은 아닙니다. 아직도 그것들은 폐허가 된 지부 내부를 배회하고 있겠지요.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_05].desc
   limit: do 850 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Lobotomy Corporation used to produce energy using creatures they dubbed ‘Abnormalities’.\nAs the term suggests, these peculiar entities ranged from unfathomably bizarre and terrifying creatures to outwardly unremarkable beings capable of causing potentially catastrophic events as a result of incorrect “management” procedures.\nAfter the Wing’s unexpected and swift collapse, most of the branches were subject to a burial protocol, trapping those creatures underground alongside the likely-oblivious employees.\nBy now, the employees have most likely passed away—but Abnormalities don’t succumb as easily. You can expect them to still prowl the ruins of these sealed facilities.
   PL: Lobotomy Corporation produkowała energię, korzystając z istot zwanych Anomaliami.\nJak głosi nazwa — od okropnych istot, których istnienia nie sposób uwierzyć, po te wyglądające zwyczajnie
150. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_06] — tytuł] E.G.O
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_06].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: E.G.O
   PL: E.G.O
151. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_06] — opis] 로보토미 코퍼레이션은 환상체를 통해 무기와 방어구를 추출하는 기술이 있었다고 전해집니다. 그것들의 내면을 물질화하는 기술로 추정되지만, 아쉽게도 현재는 소실되어 직접적인 방법을 알아낼 수는 없었습니다.\n유관 부서에서는, 수감자들의 활동 방향에 따라 그들 스스로가 E.G.O를 발현할 수 있을 것이라 예측하고 있습니다. 또한, 환상체의 E.G.O 역시 감정적 유착을 매개로 사용하는 것도 가능하리라 여기고 있습니다.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_06].desc
   limit: do 660 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Lobotomy Corporation was said to also have possessed technology allowing them to extract weapons and armor from the Abnormalities. It is speculated to be created using a technology that manifests their inner sides in material form, but unfortunately, we were unable to recover any means to replicate it.\nRelevant departments predict that your Sinners will be able to manifest E.G.O of their own depending on their course of action. In addition, they expect E.G.O from Abnormalities to be available via emotional attachment.
   PL: Podobno Lobotomy Corporation miała też technologię wydobywania broni i pancerzy z Anomalii.\nPrzypuszcza się, że była to technika materializacji ich wnętrza, ale obecnie jej nie ma i nie da się jej sprawdzić
152. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_07] — tytuł] 엔케팔린
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_07].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: Enkephalin
   PL: Enkefalina
153. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_07] — opis] 로보토미 코퍼레이션에서 생산하던 에너지를 일컫습니다.\n전력으로서 사용된다고 일반적으로 알려져 있지만, 꼭 그런 형태로만 사용되진 않습니다.\n보통 액체의 상태로 존재하는 이 초록색 물질은, 흡입시 깊은 위안감을 불러일으킨다고 알려져 있습니다. 구 L사에서는 적절한 처방을 통해 과도한 스트레스를 받은 직원에게 투여하여 업무 효율의 상승을 꾀하기도 했다고 합니다.\n하지만, 과용하거나 정제되지 않은 것에 노출될 경우에는…\n좋은 결과가 있지는 않았던 것 같군요.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_07].desc
   limit: do 670 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: This refers to the form of energy Lobotomy Corporation produced.\nWhile commonly known as a source of electricity, this is not the only use for it.\nThis green substance which normally exists in liquid form is known to induce a considerable sense of relaxation. The former L Corp. administered appropriate doses of Enkephalin to its employees suffering under great amounts of stress for increased work efficiency.\nHowever, overdose or exposure to unrefined forms of the substance…\nWell, let’s just say it didn’t seem to end well.
   PL: Tak nazywa się energia produkowana przez Lobotomy Corporation.\nPowszechnie wiadomo, że służy jako energia elektryczna, lecz nie tylko w takiej postaci.\nZwykle istnieje w stanie płynnym
154. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_08] — tytuł] 해결사
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_08].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: Fixers
   PL: Fixerzy
155. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_08] — opis] 도시에서 일어나는 수많은 사건들, 그리고 그 수만큼의 의뢰를 해결하는 것을 업으로 삼는 자들을 해결사라고 부릅니다.\n물론, 자칭하는 것 만으로 해결사가 될 수는 없으며 협회에 인가된 사무소나, 협회에 직접 방문하여 해결사 면허를 취득해야만 합니다.\n면허를 얻는 것으로 끝나는 것이 아닙니다. 그들은 말단부터 높은 등급의 해결사가 되기 위해 실적을 쌓아야만 합니다. (물론, 그런 것에 관심이 없는 자도 있습니다.)\n도시의 문제를 해결하기 위해 존재하고 있지만, 가끔은 그들 스스로가 문제가 되기도 한다는 것은 공공연한 사실이기도 합니다.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_08].desc
   limit: do 840 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: Fixers are the all-around handymen of the City, handling their clients’ various cases and incidents.\nOne doesn’t simply become a Fixer by declaring to be one; in order to acquire a Fixer license officially, one must rap on the door of an Office authorized by the Associations or visit one of the Associations in person.\nFurthermore, the license is only the beginning. To rise through the ranks and earn higher Grades, they must prove themselves through trackable performance. (Of course, some are uninterested in career advancement altogether.)\nWhile Fixers exist to solve the City’s problems, it’s no secret that these Fixers can be sources of problems themselves.
   PL: Mianem Fixerów określa się tych, którzy trudnią się rozwiązywaniem niezliczonych spraw i tyluż zleceń w Miasto.\nOczywiście, samo mianowanie się nie czyni Fixerem
156. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_09] — tytuł] 협회와 사무소
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_09].title
   limit: do 40 znaków
   EN: The Associations and Offices
   PL: Stowarzyszenia i Biura
157. [Interfejs i ustawienia [FileDownload_Desc_09] — opis] 도시에는 모든 해결사를 총괄하는 하나 협회를 주축으로, 해결사를 관리하는 커다란 단체를 협회라고 합니다. 보통 도시에서 일어나는 사건들을 각 협회의 특성에 맞게 배분하고, 협회가 또 다시 해결사에게 일을 분배하는 형태를 가지게 되죠.\n사무소는 해결사들의 모임이라는 점에서 협회와 흡사한 형태인 것 같지만, 그 규모가 작습니다. 어떠한 사무소는 협회에 직속되어 일을 하청 받기도 하고, 또 다른 사무소는 스스로 일을 찾아다니기도 하죠.\n누군가가 ‘사무소는 해결사의 수만큼 존재한다.’고 말한 것처럼, 도시에는 셀 수 없이 많은 사무소가 존재합니다. 금방 생겨났다가, 또 금방 사라지기도 하고요.
   źródło: FileDownloadDesc.json :: dataList[id=FileDownload_Desc_09].desc
   limit: do 1010 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: The Associations are major organizations that supervise and manage Fixers, and the Hana Association is at the center of all Fixer-related affairs. Cases occurring in the City are handed over to the Associations according to their specialties, and they then distribute those cases to the Fixers that are deemed to be best suited for the job.\nAn Office is similar to an Association in that it’s an organized group of Fixers, but it’s usually far smaller in scale. Some Offices are directly affiliated with an Association that issues tasks to them, while others seek clients and requests on their own. As some aptly put it: “There are as many Offices as there are Fixers.” A great number of Offices exist in the City, seeing constant waves of new entries as well as those sucked up by metaphorical riptides.
   PL: W Mieście Stowarzyszeniami nazywa się wielkie organizacje zarządzające Fixerami, zrzucone wokół jednego Stowarzyszenia zrzeszającego wszystkich Fixerów. Zwykle sprawy Miasta rozdziela się według charakteru poszczególnych Stowarzyszeń

303. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info] — tekst] 청약철회 안내
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: Refund Policy
   PL: Informacja o zwrotach
304. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Paragraph_1] — tekst] 「림버스 컴퍼니  서비스 이용약관」을 근거로 한 청약철회 정책에 대해 안내드립니다.
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Paragraph_1].content
   limit: do 160 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: We would like to inform you of our Refund Policy as per the information written in the 「Limbus Company Terms of Service」.
   PL: Informujemy o zasadach zwrotu wynikających z „Regulaminu Usługi Limbus Company”.
305. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_1] — tekst] 1. 회사와 유료 콘텐츠의 구매에 관한 계약을 체결한 회원은 구매계약일과 콘텐츠 이용 가능일 중 늦은 날부터 7일 이내에 별도의 수수료‧위약금 등의 부담 없이 청약철회를 할 수 있습니다.
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_1].content
   limit: do 350 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: 1. Members who have signed a contract for the purchase of paid Contents with the Company can withdraw their subscription without the burden of additional fees or penalties within 7 days from the later of the date of the purchase contract or the date the Contents is available.
   PL: 1. Członek, który zawarł z firmą umowę zakupu treści płatnych, może odstąpić od umowy bez opłat i kar w ciągu 7 dni od późniejszej z dat: dnia zawarcia umowy i dnia udostępnienia treści.
306. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2] — tekst] 2. 회원은 다음 각 호에 해당하는 경우에는 회사의 의사에 반하여 제1항에 따른 청약철회를 할 수 없습니다. 다만, 가분적 콘텐츠로 구성된 구매계약의 경우에는 가분적 콘텐츠 중 다음 각 호에 해당하지 아니하는 나머지 부분에 대하여는 그러하지 아니합니다.
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2].content
   limit: do 410 znaków
   EN: 2. Members cannot withdraw their subscriptions under Paragraph 1 against the will of the Company in the following cases. However, in the case of a purchase contract consisting of divisible Contents, this does not apply to the remaining parts of divisible Contents that do not fall under each of the following subparagraphs.
   PL: 2. Członek nie może odstąpić od umowy z ust. 1 wbrew woli firmy, gdy zachodzi którakolwiek z niżej wymienionych okoliczności; jednakże w umowach z treściami podzielnymi odstąpienie dotyczy tylko części, której nie wykorzystano.
307. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_1] — tekst] (1) 구매 즉시 사용되거나 적용되는 콘텐츠
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_1].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: (1) Contents used or applied immediately upon purchase
   PL: (1) Treści używane lub stosowane natychmiast po zakupie
308. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_2] — tekst] (2) 추가혜택이 제공되는 경우에 그 추가 혜택이 사용된 콘텐츠
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_2].content
   limit: do 120 znaków
   EN: (2) Contents in which additional benefits are used, if additional benefits are provided
   PL: (2) Treści, w których wykorzystano dodatkowe korzyści, jeśli je zapewniono
309. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_3] — tekst] (3) 개봉 행위를 사용으로 볼 수 있거나 개봉 시 효용이 결정되는 콘텐츠의 개봉 행위가 있는 경우
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_2_3].content
   limit: do 160 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: (3) If there is an act of opening Contents that can be seen as a use or whose utility is determined at the time of opening
   PL: (3) Treści, których otwarcie można uznać za użycie albo których użyteczność zostaje ustalona przy otwarciu
310. [Interfejs i ustawienia [retunPolicy_Info_Desc_3] — tekst] 3. 회사는 제2항 각 호의 규정에 따라 청약철회가 불가능한 콘텐츠의 경우에는 그 사실을 회원이 쉽게 알 수 있는 곳에 명확하게 표시하고, 해당 콘텐츠의 시험사용 상품을 제공(한시적 이용의 허용, 체험용 제공 등)하거나 이에 대한 제공이 곤란한 경우에는 콘텐츠에 관한 정보를 제공함으로써 회원의 청약철회의 권리행사가 방해받지 아니하도록 조치합니다.
   źródło: ReturnPolicy.json :: dataList[id=retunPolicy_Info_Desc_3].content
   limit: do 560 znaków
   tokeny: zachowaj dokładnie: \n
   EN: 3. In the case of Contents that cannot be withdrawn under each subparagraph of Paragraph 2, the Company clearly displays the fact in a place where Members can easily find out and provides trial use products for the Contents (temporary use permitted, experience only) or if it is difficult to provide it, the Company provides information about the Contents so that the exercise of the Member's right to withdraw the subscription is not hindered.
   PL: 3. W przypadku treści, których nie można odstąpić zgodnie z ust. 2, firma wyraźnie oznacza to w miejscu łatwo dostrzegalnym dla członka i podejmuje środki zapobiegające szkodom.
